- Orthogonal-bend E_z subpixel material coefficients use positive physical dual-face area weights before the existing center metric Hodge. Analytic layered, graded and coefficient-gradient controls pass; Cartesian and zero-curvature arithmetic is preserved, while mapped end-to-end adjoints and bent-antenna mesh qualification remain open.

- The 0.625 mm physical microstrip outer-grid control retains five-frequency, two-drive unfitted E/H records and validates complete comparison inputs. Outer-grid impedance/beta differences are below 0.007%/0.003%; the separate continuum impedance failure remains open.

- Independent coupled-line validation retains four complete physical drives at five frequencies and six measurement intervals. Common/differential physical V/I impedance and propagation agree within 0.0129%/0.00970% on the matched discrete grid; continuum, finite-loss attenuation and typed modal-port S remain separately unqualified.

- Capability-boundary audits retain actual source-bound rejection events and passing test node IDs, separately from static matching assertions. CPU controls record 39 new/changed raising sites; 56 new/changed boundaries still await executed negative coverage, and unsupported combinations remain unsupported.

- The printed-line network validation driver accepts an explicit transverse refinement radius and records actual prepared node arrays and hashes. Nine CPU contracts preserve physical conductors, modal reference planes, external nodes and PML under local refinement; fine full-band modal-S execution and convergence remain pending.
- Independent printed-line references can reuse explicitly identified completed drives while executing missing drives in fresh directories. Reuse checks exact XML, timestep, step count, original timing and source/build provenance; partial traces cannot be appended. Recovery evidence binds every retained raw file and distinguishes the original execution driver from the new orchestration driver.
- Research-only split-plane local CN/IMEX retains true small cut intervals at the original bulk CFL. Independent CPU full-matrix, positive-energy, spectral and harmonic controls pass, including 85-digit comparisons and solved copper trace midpoints. CUDA time-domain execution and native 3D curved-copper integration remain unqualified.
- Unsupported trainable source-time scalars, magnetic conductivity, domain/grid inputs and point-observer coordinates reject before scalar or NumPy conversion. Fixed inputs retain their behavior; registered electric/copper material parameters, RF excitation amplitude, circuit waveform tensors and batch source scales retain their existing tensor paths. This validation does not enable mapped adjoints or new parameter derivatives.
- Cartesian conduction-loss material gradients retain live conductivity coefficients without requiring a copper foil. Native batch export preserves those coefficients and their autograd ownership through repeated runs and close; four public GPU controls cover detached finite differences, Graph, checkpoint/disk resume and post-close backward. Final-source full regression and mapped-material admission remain separate requirements.
- Printed-line and dipole loss diagnostics can record exact native electric planes and reduce one frequency at a time through shared bulk, split-trace and film work. Six CUDA parity controls cover eager/Graph execution, native-point/full-E agreement and Result persistence. Full antenna spatial convergence and measured peak memory remain pending.
- Independent printed-line reference audits re-reduce retained raw spectra, check actual coordinates/times/source identities and expose unwanted ground-mode symmetry. Completed fine references retain failed mesh increments and contaminated scalar-mode estimates; reference process completion does not qualify fine native propagation or attenuation.
- A research normal-incidence split-plane adapter couples genuine prepared CUDA background kernels to independent local E/H and passive two-face copper states. Six actual one-step operator controls pass for both polarizations and true small cuts; physical scattering, lifecycle extensions and arbitrary curved copper remain unqualified and publicly guarded.
- Maintained regression execution supports detached source-bound pytest shards, real process and test deadlines, original per-phase results and strict resume. CPU lifecycle probes cover failure, skip/xfail, crash, node/shard timeout, interrupted controller recovery, input changes and interpreter shutdown hangs; a completed node alone cannot hide a failed worker exit.

- A research-only off-grid 1D copper diagnostic uses independent split fields, positive side control volumes and the shared passive two-face CN material solve. CPU local-energy and independent harmonic controls pass; CUDA time controls, production 3D integration and general curved-surface qualification remain pending.

- One-sided CopperFoil field queries use actual two-sided E spectra and same-side H/normal-E samples on Cartesian and mapped grids, with explicit interface-side selection. Nonuniform supports, persistence and material-gradient controls pass; settled-CW charge reconstruction converges under three meshes. Native monitors through jumps and general cut cells remain separately bounded.

- Component/frequency-selected `Result.query_fields` returns named `[frequency, point]` native spectra and explicit Cu Et owner/side/representation. Actual per-face sampling provenance replaces phase/scale inference in this strict mode; real instantaneous fields and derived planes cannot masquerade as native spectra. Cartesian real-field eager/Graph, checkpoint, persistence and live material-VJP controls pass on a bounded 11-case GPU matrix. Initial support is fixed float64 positions, nonperiodic total fields, raw source normalization and the none window; mapped/complex-bank/coordinate/weighted/reference extensions remain explicit capability debt. Full PCB mode, mesh and attenuation acceptance is separate.


- FDTD construction and scene refresh reject trainable frequencies, time controls and execution/sampling controls before scalar conversion. Fixed generators are consumed once; registered material, source-amplitude and circuit tensor paths remain available. CPU integration covers constructor and mutated-control rejection without enabling mapped material adjoints.

- Printed-line copper-loss diagnostics preserve owned writable coefficient storage and support two real broadband basis drives with independent raw-spectrum re-reduction. The first 64 ns stripline pilot passes source/ROI/checkpoint and functional power controls; its remaining 6.74–7.41% heat-relative imbalance does not qualify attenuation, mesh convergence or modal scattering.

- Antenna development runs can persist lossless verified gzip Result artifacts through anonymous RAM, with pre-solve raw-size resource admission and unchanged public snapshot schema. CPU integrity controls and a genuine CUDA-result bitwise round trip pass; large full-band resource and physical qualification remain separate.

- Fixed source/observer geometry, monitor frequencies/quadrature, subpixel counts, boundary controls, PEC/foil topology and port reference planes reject trainable inputs before conversion. All 183 CPU integration contracts pass; registered differentiable material, source amplitude, impedance and geometry paths retain their tensor ownership.

- FDTD offers explicit float64 native detached forward fields and float64 sparse spectral accumulation for fixed-grid scalar bulk materials and two-sided copper with direct Norton ports. The bounded path requires eager single-device all-face dense CPML, one frequency and supported sparse monitors; unsupported execution/material/gradient combinations reject before field updates. CPU268, public GPU3 plus core GPU4, and all52 original default-precision CUDA parity controls pass on the same candidate sources. FP64 adjoints, Graph/batch/distributed execution and mapped-material admission remain unqualified.

- Result snapshots preserve repeated Tensor object identity during recursive GPU-to-host conversion, avoiding duplicate native-plane aliases while retaining distinct views and field representations. Actual CUDA persistence failures are repaired; unchanged-schema bitwise round trips and real32-step monitor payload comparisons pass.

- Planar real-field CopperFoil CUDA steps use a fused FP64 electric kernel and a companion kernel for begin-step snapshots, magnetic correction and side-spectrum accumulation, with graph capture support. Field-dtype and indexed-add semantics match the torch reference: E1's FP64 elementwise gate passes and fp32 writes differ by 0 ulp; E1b qualifies spectra normwise. Paired/phase and material-trace ADE entries retain the torch step; CPU and adjoint/replay arithmetic remain torch. The internal `WITWIN_MAXWELL_COPPER_KERNEL=torch` switch retains the reference path and historical pins; no re-pins were made. Local sm_86 captured copper-plus-port timing reached 0.294 ms/step and 54 kernels/step, meeting the 0.35 ms/step target in observed clean fused windows. The paired series measured torch at median 0.918 ms/step with 430–438 kernels/step and median paired speedup 3.13x; intermittent contention is recorded per trial in the qualification report.
- Conformal thin-sheet CUDA chord solves use deterministic cooperative CSR kernels for both the system and preconditioner, including feed columns spanning multiple chords. Each cooperative CSR plan selects L once at preparation as the smallest power of two at least ceil(mean row nnz / 2), clamped to [1, 8]; L=1 uses a sequential FMA loop. The existing thin-sheet kernel selector retains the unchanged torch polynomial as the reference. The fused implementation uses three kernels per nonfinal Chebyshev inner step, one on the final step, and two setup kernels per outer pass; degree three requires 19 kernels including initialization. `scripts/dev/fdtd/d3b/chord_kernel_bench.py --scenario copper_patch` (or `copper_board_60k`) prints CUDA-event JSON timings for all four lane specializations and the same system/preconditioner tensor products, using 200 calls after warmup. The fused thin-sheet gates passed: overhead patch 1.34× (gate 1.5×) and board 1.83× (gate 2.0×), and the thin-sheet suite passes.
- Printed-line loss controls separate PEC with dielectric conductivity from copper without dielectric loss, retaining fixed source stamping, longitudinal grid and physical V/I observation functionals during transverse refinement. Ninety CPU contracts and five exact compiled-source/material/geometry preflights pass; actual material controls and attenuation convergence are tracked separately.

- Independent printed-line flux diagnostics retain native electric/magnetic products before cell averaging and actual two-sided copper traces. Forty-one analytic/Green/CN CPU controls and three retained64ns data sets recover the independent1W V/I normalization within6.2ppm and reduce heat-relative closure below0.44% for dielectric/combined cases and1.36% for copper alone. Public flux integration and its GPU/gradient qualification remain a separate stage.

- Fixed-chart electrical material adjoints support scalar permittivity/conductivity and fixed-order two-sided copper thickness/conductivity through registered PyTorch parameter leaves. Explicit fixed grid/time/feed/observations and single-device real-field execution are required; unsupported semantic uses and unaccounted module leaves reject. All24 public GPU contracts and475 main CPU integration contracts pass; private historical failures remain retained. PrimaryFP64 adjoints and mapped native batching remain outside this scope.

- Bounded printed-line source-superposition checks use an explicit bilinear power scale derived from the actual independent source fields, native endpoints, physical face areas and both copper traces. They retain the original signed-entering-power diagnostics and thresholds; this functional check does not qualify weak-flux relative precision or attenuation.

- Regression identity reports include dispatcher-loaded shared libraries from `torch.ops.loaded_libraries`, with canonical paths and actual binary SHA256. Real CPU library controls cover libraries absent from Python modules, repeated/alias loads, distinct libraries sharing a filename, and missing-file rejection.

- Fixed-k Bloch slab TFSF supports broadband source times through a complex 1D Yee incident line with the grid's discrete transverse wavenumbers, normal spacing and time step, CPML at both ends, and phased corrections to both field banks. Checkpoint/resume preserves its fields, CPML memory, source clock and spectrum. `Result.metadata["tfsf_incident_reference"]` exposes the electric spectrum at the lower TFSF plane with physical Yee observer labels; its soft drive retains integer-step sampling pending separate raw-exact qualification; `reflection_transmission(result, ...)` can use it without a separate incident run. CW, zero-k and non-Bloch source paths are unchanged. CPU dispersion, confinement and checkpoint controls pass; the GPU acceptance runs passed, with the passivity guard warn-only on parity, CW continuity and cutoff; graph/resume qualification remains pending. Beams, mode and custom-field sources and soft plane waves remain CW-only under fixed k.
- Fixed-k_parallel broadband postprocessing exposes native-Yee-plane Floquet TE/TM amplitudes, propagating-order power-normalized complex reflection/transmission against an empty-cell reference, and mask-preserving fixed-angle label resampling in PyTorch. Labels default to a 5% incident light-line margin. Acceptance pulses suppress spectral amplitude below 2e-8 at that margin; empty-cell diagnostics retain electric/magnetic energy histories with consistent weighted peak normalization. Off-grid oblique-slab Airy refinement and long-CW continuity acceptance drivers are provided; their GPU acceptance runs passed, with the passivity guard warn-only on parity, CW continuity and cutoff. `scripts/dev/fdtd/bloch_floquet_acceptance/fixed_k_label_phase_probe.py --device cpu|cuda --output <json>` measures the exported incident spectrum's one-step label phase against an in-process label revert, using the incident line alone on CPU and the full native scene on CUDA.
- Experimental conformal sheet gap feeds use one longitudinal series branch per
  transverse column, partitioned gap quadrature, native port accumulation and
  checkpoint state. Feed axes must be Cartesian and in-plane in the supporting
  facet; non-Cartesian feed directions are rejected. The gap and its one-cell
  longitudinal margins must stay on one planar facet. Column spectra and
  redistribution variance power are exposed
  in `PortData.metadata["sheet_gap"]`. One-cell-strip equivalence passes the
  0.2% gate at both tested grids. In the task-2 cavity checks, wider feeds differ
  from the single-edge native branch by up to 3.44e-3 in Z_in, partly converging
  discretization and partly a ~1% transverse redistribution power term.
  The task-3 axis-aligned open dipole control differs by 0.6762% at lambda/40.
  Passivity is qualified on the scheme's
  modified discrete energy (non-increasing, decaying with a resistive load); the
  physical E^2 + H^2 sum is not a leapfrog invariant. The F2 rotation gate fails:
  for normals (0, 0.025, 1) and (0, sin 30 degrees, cos 30 degrees), maximum band
  Z_in differences are 212.77%/185.23% at lambda/40 and 168.03%/165.82% at
  lambda/60, with resonance shifts of 18.12–20.16%; D_max differences stay below
  0.054 dB. F2b curved self-convergence also fails: common-resonance and upper-edge
  impedance changes grow from 178.6847/326.0230 ohm to 178.9352/395.6786 ohm, while
  the lambda/60-to-lambda/80 D_max change is 0.01641 dB. Driven antenna accuracy
  remains unqualified. With the rim fix (partial rim chords rejected), F2 resonance shifts fall from 18-20% to about 2% at lambda/40 (0.6-2.7% at lambda/60) and converge, but the Z_in and 0.5% resonance gates are still not met; they track the rim staircase, and a sub-cell rim treatment would be needed. F2/F2b stay strict expected failures. F3 exact field-side power balance passes at lambda/60:
  worst residuals are 0.02849% for the tilted dipole, 0.001689% for the tilted
  patch and 0.015023% for the curved dipole (1% gate).
- Provider epoch v3 freezes the 30-module dense-core/default acceptance scope, all 369 reviewed package deltas and an isolated byte-identical core copy. Its 395 admitted tests pass across 42 source-bound GPU-1 shards; the receipt, exact archive/readback and reviews preserve the infrastructure retry and explicit artifact/multi-device exclusions. Other solver families remain source-hashed and unqualified; refinement and consumer requalification are separate work.

- Native float32 FDTD adjoints gather collocation, TFSF auxiliary samples and coincident point-monitor seeds in fixed orders without floating-point atomics in these accumulations. Static sample and point-cell plans stay on device. Gradients are bitwise repeatable on GPU; the TFSF adjoint step costs about 7% more.
  MaterialRegion density and Box parameter-field sampling use shared float32/float64 CUDA trilinear kernels whose forward and coordinate gradient are bitwise equal to the border-padded trilinear `grid_sample`; the texture gradient is an ordered CSR gather built on first backward (no float atomics), so density and parameter-field gradients are bitwise repeatable. Non-finite queries follow `grid_sample`. Double backward through this sampling is not supported and raises.
